# Căn Nhà Âm Dương: Oan Hồn Dưới Gốc Mít — Bộ 90 Prompts Tối Ưu Hóa (15 Cảnh × 6 Clips × 10s = 15 Phút)

- **Giọng Người Dẫn Chuyện (`VOICE_LOCK_NARRATOR_CNHADG`):** Cố định 100% một giọng Nam 42–48 tuổi chuẩn miền Bắc (deep warm low-baritone ~108–110 Hz, slightly husky, natural chest resonance, kể chuyện ma đêm khuya, tốc độ chuẩn `125–145 từ/phút`).
- **Tối ưu độ dài lời dẫn / thoại:** 100% các clip (90/90) có độ dài câu nói từ **12–18 từ tiếng Việt**, khớp hoàn hảo trong khung 10 giây mà không bao giờ bị cắt chữ hay đọc vội.
- **Cấu trúc chuẩn 9 Phần (`[1. FORMAT & MODEL]` → `[9. CONTINUITY & QUALITY RULES]`):** Đồng nhất chuyển động máy quay đơn trục (không mâu thuẫn flycam/gimbal), khóa `media_id` nhân vật và khớp nối `START FRAME` / `END FRAME`.

---

## Cảnh 01 - Clip 1 (`canh-01/clip-1.md`)

```markdown
[1. FORMAT & MODEL]
- Duration & Aspect Ratio: 10 seconds (0.0s–10.0s), full-frame 16:9 widescreen, 24 fps, 180-degree cinema shutter.
- Model & Aesthetics: `omni_flash` (Google Flow Omni 10s Ingredients Reference-to-Video), photorealistic live-action Vietnamese supernatural folk-horror cinema.
- Visual Look & Grade: Authentic Northern Vietnamese architecture and rural atmosphere, natural skin textures and pores, damp mist, realistic shadows, flickering warm oil-lamp/tungsten amber contrasted against cold midnight cyan-blue darkness, subtle 35mm film grain. Single continuous 10-second camera shot (`Shot 01.1`, film timeline `00:00–00:10`).

[2. CHARACTER & VOICE LOCK]
Use attached reference images (`media_id`) strictly to lock facial identity, age, hairstyle, and wardrobe inside the live scene. Only the listed characters appear in frame.
- @Binh = Nguyễn Hòa Bình (media_id: a1a78643-ce72-4217-86c7-b53b927e637a | age 21): Northern Vietnamese male senior university student, 172 cm, thin pale build, messy short black hair, tired dark eyes with visible dark circles. Wardrobe: faded charcoal-grey hoodie over a wrinkled white T-shirt, dark slim jeans, worn sneakers, carrying a black backpack. Character Voice Reference (VOICE_LOCK_BINH_CNHADG): 21-year-old native Standard Northern Vietnamese young male voice (~135 Hz tenor-baritone), trembling, anxious, guilt-ridden and breathless in horror moments (giọng nam trẻ 21 tuổi chuẩn miền Bắc, run rẩy, nghẹn ngào).
- Narrator (Người Dẫn Chuyện) = Off-screen 42–48-year-old native Standard Northern Vietnamese male storyteller (VOICE_LOCK_NARRATOR_CNHADG): Deep warm low-baritone (~108–110 Hz), slightly husky, natural chest resonance, intimate late-night Vietnamese ghost-story storytelling style. Target speaking speed: 125–145 Vietnamese words per minute (this clip: 125–132 Vietnamese words/minute with 0.40s natural dramatic pauses at ellipses (…); emotional tone: calm, hushed, mysterious, intimate late-night ghost-story delivery). Reuse the same TTS voice ID / acoustic voice reference (VOICE_LOCK_NARRATOR_CNHADG) across all 90 clips, relying on this full acoustic profile rather than a text label alone.

[3. SCENE & SPATIAL LOCK]
- Scene & Chronology: Scene 01/15 (Chuyến Xe Khách Đêm Khuya & Món Nợ Bốn Trăm Triệu), Clip 1/6 (Clip #01/90 | 00:00–00:10). Story Time: 23:30 Đêm khuya trên quốc lộ về quê nội (00:00–01:00).
- Location & Architecture: Lone sleeper coach bus crawling along a misty Northern Vietnamese rural highway and the dimly lit window seat inside the bus cabin (plus brief phone cutaway to @BaPhuong's kitchen).
- Spatial Geometry Lock: Inside the dimly lit sleeper bus cabin by the rain-streaked glass window where @Binh clutches his black backpack as amber highway lights sweep past.
- Key Scene Props: Sleeper coach bus window streaked with rain, worn plush passenger seat, black backpack, glowing smartphone screen showing a liquidated trading account.

[4. DRAMATIC OBJECTIVE]
Opening aerial shot gliding over dark, fog-shrouded Northern Vietnamese rice paddies at midnight and descending toward the rain-streaked window of a lone sleeper coach bus where @Binh sits huddled.

[5. CAMERA & TIMED ACTION]
- Single Coherent Camera Plan (0.0s–10.0s): 24mm stabilized aerial drone slow descending push-in. One physically plausible camera movement on a single consistent axis from 0.0s to 10.0s; zero contradictory camera rigs, zero sudden zooms, zero jump cuts.
- 0.0s–2.5s (Opening Establishing Beat): Opening shot of the film (no previous clip required). Wide aerial view above dark misty Northern Vietnamese rice fields as a lone coach bus rolls along the highway.
- 2.5s–7.5s (Core Dramatic Progression): Opening aerial shot gliding over dark, fog-shrouded Northern Vietnamese rice paddies at midnight and descending toward the rain-streaked window of a lone sleeper coach bus where @Binh sits huddled.
- 7.5s–10.0s (Outgoing Continuity Settle): Camera pushes in close to the rain-streaked sleeper bus window revealing @Binh huddled inside. Hold a steady 0.5s visual and audio settle at 9.5s–10.0s to bridge cleanly into Clip 01.2.

[6. NARRATION / DIALOGUE]
- Word Budget & Pacing Check: 18 Vietnamese words (~8.6s active speech window at 125–145 WPM), finishing naturally at 8.8s before the 10.0s clip ends without rushing or truncation.
- Turn 1 [0.8s–8.8s | 18 Vietnamese words | 125–132 Vietnamese words/minute] Off-Screen Male Narrator (Người Dẫn Chuyện — VOICE_LOCK_NARRATOR_CNHADG | speaking off-screen in a 42–48-year-old native Standard Northern Vietnamese deep warm low-baritone, slightly husky, intimate late-night ghost-story style; all on-screen characters keep their mouths naturally closed): "Ôm món nợ bốn trăm triệu không lối thoát, Bình bỏ trốn về căn nhà hoang ông nội."
- Lip-Sync & Mouth Lock: Off-screen narration ONLY. All on-screen characters (@Binh) keep their mouths naturally closed throughout 0.0s–10.0s, expressing emotion through eyes, breathing, and posture.
- Spoken Audio Rule: Speak ONLY the Vietnamese words inside quotation marks ("..."). Never read prompt instructions, @tags, character IDs, timestamps, or stage directions aloud.

[7. HORROR SOUND DESIGN]
- Synchronized Scene Audio (0.0s–10.0s): Tiếng động cơ xe khách rầm rì trầm đục xuyên màn mưa đêm và tiếng gió rít ngoài cánh đồng.
- Mix & Silence Discipline: Keep spoken Standard Northern Vietnamese narration/dialogue clearly audible in the foreground above environmental foley. Use strategic quiet tension, avoid excessive music or generic jump-scare stingers, and maintain a smooth 0.15s ambient room-tone transition at 0.0s and 10.0s.

[8. START FRAME & END FRAME]
- START FRAME (0.0s): Wide aerial view above dark misty Northern Vietnamese rice fields as a lone coach bus rolls along the highway.
- END FRAME (10.0s): Camera pushes in close to the rain-streaked sleeper bus window revealing @Binh huddled inside.

[9. CONTINUITY & QUALITY RULES]
- Preserve character facial identity, hair, body proportions, and costume across the entire 10-second shot; hands must have 5 natural fingers and grip props with realistic weight.
- Never switch narrator gender, accent (must remain 100% Standard Northern Vietnamese / giọng chuẩn miền Bắc), or vocal age/timbre.
- Full-bleed 16:9 image with no subtitles, no captions, no on-screen text, no watermarks, no black letterbox bars, and never flash or cut to a studio reference portrait backdrop.
```
